                             與市場週期奮力博弈，更在企業發展中錘煉戰略     encountered since listing. In the face of these challenges, we
                             定力。在此，我謹代表集團及董事會，向在行業     have not only been striving to navigate the headwinds of market
                             調整期中始終關注集團並給予我們信任與支持的     cycles, but also fortifying our own strategic focuses along our
                             每一位股東，致以誠摯的謝意。            development. On behalf of the Group and the Board, I wish to

                             內採取了控貨去庫存戰略，力求穩定渠道價格體     Group’s financial performance in 2025 came under considerable

                             一次「清瘀止痛」的必要調整，目的是優化渠道健    choice to “proactively recalibrate and build momentum for the

                            EMBRACING A NEW CHAPTER

面對行業的深度調整，我們沒有被短期波動打亂       Faced with the profound challenges in the industry, we refused
節奏，而是着眼於行業核心痛點，大膽創新，破       to be derailed by short-term fluctuations. Instead, we set
局啟航，將挑戰轉化為機遇。我們回歸經營本        our sights on the industry’s core pain points, embraced bold
質，以創新精神構建超級鏈接、超級產品、超級       innovation, and charted a course to break through – transforming
渠道、超級傳播、超級組織、超級品牌、超級體       challenges into opportunities. Returning to the very fundamentals
驗。                          of our business, we have channelled an innovative spirit into
                            building seven pillars of excellence: premier connections,
                            premier products, premier channels, premier marketing, premier
                            organisation, premier brands, and premier experiences.

    局。經過2025 年的實踐與驗證，「萬商聯         – and long-term interests of our distributors, this new-

未來展望：韌者行遠，向新而行             LOOKING AHEAD: GOING FURTHER WITH
                           RESILIENCE, STRIVING TOWARDS A NEW ERA

展望2026 年，適逢「十五五」規劃開局之年，白   Looking ahead to 2026, the inaugural year of China’s 15th Five-
酒行業已從規模擴張轉向價值提升。從業三十餘      Year Plan, the baijiu industry has pivoted from scale expansion
載，我見證過白酒行業數次調整週期。每一次都      towards value creation. With over thirty years in this industry,
是一場突如其來的「大考」，考驗的是企業的韌      I have witnessed several cycles of downturns in the baijiu
性和敢於以創新思維應對挑戰的能力。歷史經驗      industry. Each one has arrived as a sudden and searching test
已告訴我們，白酒行業從來不是一條直線上升的      of our capabilities to address challenges with an innovative
坦途，而是波浪式前進、在震蕩中進化的長青事      mindset. History has shown us that the baijiu industry has never
業。                         followed a straight and unobstructed upward path – it is a time-
                           honoured business that advances in cycles and evolves through
                           turbulence.

底層邏輯從未改變。珍酒李渡集團經歷過風雨，      the fundamentals underpinning the industry’s upward trajectory
才能更懂得如何在逆風中前行。我們現在的每一      remain unchanged. It is precisely because ZJLD Group has
次主動調整、每一項渠道變革，都是在為下一個      weathered the storms that we have come to understand how to
週期的爆發積蓄力量。回望2025年集團在渠道和    press forward against the wind. Every proactive recalibration

的發展軌道。萬商聯盟的模式紅利正持續釋放，      is an accumulation of strength for the resurgence that the next

                             概覽                           Overview
                             我們是一家致力於釀造高品質白酒產品的中國領        We are a leading baijiu company in China devoted to offering
                             先白酒公司。我們生產及銷售醬香型、兼香型及        high-quality baijiu products. We produce and sell sauce aroma,

                             引擎。                          serving as our major growth engine.

                             於2025年，白酒行業面臨若干挑戰，特別是白酒      In 2025, the baijiu industry faced certain challenges, notably
                             消費需求自2025年第二季度由於若干反腐敗政策      with a continuously weakened consumption demand resulting
                             的推出而持續下滑。為應對當前經濟環境的該等        from certain anti-graft policies in the second quarter of 2025. In
                             挑戰及白酒消費需求的下滑，我們致力於減少渠        response to such challenges within the current economic climate

於2025年，我們繼續開展創新嘗試以應對行業挑     Throughout 2025, we continued our innovative attempts to

                             外匯風險                          Foreign Exchange Exposure
                             截至2025 年12 月31 日止年度，本集團在中國開   During the year ended December 31, 2025, the Group
                             展其業務，而交易以人民幣結算。本集團中國附         conducted its business in China with the transactions settled in




珍酒李渡集團有限公司 ZJLD GROUP INC
                             屬公司的功能貨幣為人民幣。因本集團相關業務         Renminbi. The functional currency of the Group’s subsidiaries
                             在其各自之營運上形成自然對沖機制，外匯風險         in the PRC is RMB. There is a natural hedge mechanism in place
                             的影響很低，因此認為無需以任何金融工具作對         during the course of its respective business operation and the
                             沖用途。為加強整體風險管理，本集團將繼續留         impact of the foreign exchange risk is low, therefore no financial
                             意外匯風險，並採取審慎措施緩解外匯風險且於         instruments for hedging purposes are considered necessary.
                             必要時採取適當行動。                    To enhance overall risk management, the Group will continue
                                                           to keep track of the foreign exchange risk and take prudent
                                                           measures to mitigate exchange risk, and take appropriate action
                                                           where necessary.

董事會欣然提呈本董事會報告以及本集團截至           The Board is pleased to present this Directors’ Report together
2025年12月31日止年度的綜合財務報表。         with the consolidated financial statements of the Group for the
                               year ended December 31, 2025.

主要業務                           PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES
本公司及其附屬公司主要在中國從事釀造、生產          The Company and its subsidiaries are principally engaged in the

首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃               POST-IPO EQUITY INCENTIVE PLAN
首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃於2023年4月11日    The Post-IPO Equity Incentive Plan was approved by way of
經股東決議案獲批准，並自上市日期起生效。該       shareholders’ resolution on April 11, 2023, with effect from
計劃的目的是激勵合資格參與者並就彼等對本集       the Listing Date. The purpose of this plan is to incentivize and
團作出的貢獻給予獎勵，使彼等的利益與本公司       reward eligible participants for their contribution to the Group
利益一致，藉此鼓勵彼等努力提升本公司價值。       and to align their interests with that of the Company, so as to
                            encourage them to work towards enhancing the value of the




                                                                                                年度報告 ANNUAL REPORT 2025
                            Company.

                             聯盟商權益支付計劃                    ALLIANCE RETAILERS BENEFITS PLAN
                             於2025 年9月，本公司通過其全資附屬公司珍酒     In September 2025, the Company, through its wholly owned
                             商貿採納聯盟商權益支付計劃，該計劃旨在激勵        subsidiary, Zhenjiu Commercial Trading, adopted the Alliance
                             合資格聯盟商，以促進本公司的長期可持續增長        Retailers Benefits Plan, which is designed to motivate the
                             及發展。聯盟商權益支付計劃項下的經濟受益單        Eligible Alliance Retailers for long-term sustainable growth and
                             位將使用來源於本公司現有已發行股份的經濟收        development of the Company. The Economic Benefits Units
                             益兌現。因此，聯盟商權益支付計劃不構成上市        under the Alliance Retailers Benefits Plan will be satisfied by
                             規則第17章所規管的涉及發行新股份的計劃。        using the proceeds from existing issued Shares of the Company.
                                                          As such, the Alliance Retailers Benefits Plan does not constitute
                                                          a scheme involving issuance of new Shares governed under
                                                          Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules.

                             聯盟商權益支付計劃的主要條款概述如下：          The principal terms of the Alliance Retailers Benefits Plan are
                                                          summarized as follows:

                             (a)   目的                     (a)    Purpose

                                   擴展本集團經銷網絡及品牌覆蓋範圍的貢            Alliance Retailers for their contribution to the expansion
                                   獻，透過為其提供與股份、就股份所作其            of the Group’s distribution network and brand coverage,
                                   他分派及 ╱ 或股份增值掛鈎的經濟利益，          to align their long-term interests with those of the Group

                                   並進一步激勵和挽留合資格聯盟商以及吸            distributions paid on Shares and/or increase in the value

                             企業管治常規                        CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES
                             本公司深知企業管治是卓越運營的基石。我們不         The Company understands that corporate governance is
                             斷完善企業管治架構，高度重視內外部企業風險         the cornerstone of excellent operations. We have been
                             管理及合規工作。我們亦一直致力於加強董事          continuously refining our corporate governance structure,
                             會層面的ESG工作，同時密切監察持份者對我們        placing high importance on enterprise risk management and
                             ESG工作的期望及要求。展望未來，我們的目標        compliance both internally and externally. We have also been
                             是不斷優化我們的管治體系，提高管治水平，滿         persistently working on enhancing ESG efforts at board level,
                             足監管要求，並營造一個透明、多元化和值得信         while closely monitoring the expectations and demands of
                             賴的企業環境。                       stakeholders regarding our ESG work. Looking forward, we
                                                           aim to continuously optimize our governance system, enhance
                                                           governance standards, meet regulatory requirements, and foster
                                                           a transparent, diverse and trustworthy corporate environment.

                             本公司的企業管治常規以企業管治守則所載的原         The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on the
                             則及守則條文為基礎，且本公司已採納企業管治         principles and code provisions as set out in the CG Code and
                             守則的守則條文作為其本身的企業管治守則。董         the Company has adopted the code provisions of the CG Code
                             事會認為，於報告期內，本公司已遵守企業管治         as its own code of corporate governance. The Board is of the
                             守則所載的所有守則條文。董事會將定期檢討及         view that the Company has complied with all code provisions as
                             加強其企業管治常規，以確保本公司繼續符合企         set out in the CG Code during the Reporting Period. The Board
                             業管治守則的規定。                     will periodically review and enhance its corporate governance
                                                           practices to ensure that the Company continues to meet the
                                                           requirements of the CG Code.

遵守董事進行證券交易的標準守則                 COMPLIANCE WITH THE MODEL CODE FOR
                                SECURITIES TRANSACTIONS BY DIRECTORS
本公司已採納標準守則，作為其本身就其董事及           The Company has adopted the Model Code as its own code of
可能掌握本公司內幕消息的相關僱員進行本公司           conduct regarding the transactions of securities of the Company

                            董事會多元化政策                                       BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY
                            本集團採納的董事會多元化政策載列我們實現及                          The board diversity policy that the Group has adopted sets out
                            維持董事會多元化的方法。根據該政策，我們在                          our approach to achieve and maintain diversity of the Board.

                            體業務狀況及業務戰略、預期營運資金要求及未     expected results of operations, cash flow and financial position,

                            意見                            OPINION
                            我們已審計列載於第 130 頁至 225 頁的珍酒李渡   We have audited the consolidated financial statements of ZJLD
                            集團有限公司（以下簡稱「貴公司」）及其附屬公        Group Inc (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”)
                            司（以下統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表，該等        set out on pages 130 to 225, which comprise the consolidated
                            財務報表包括於2025 年12 月31 日的綜合財務狀   statement of financial position as at 31 December 2025, the
                            況表與截至該日止年度的綜合損益及其他全面收         consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive
                            入表、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表以及附         income, the consolidated statement of changes in equity and the
                            註，包括重大會計政策資料及其他解釋資料。          consolidated cash flow statement for the year then ended and
                                                          notes, comprising material accounting policy information and
                                                          other explanatory information.

                            我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據國際會計準         In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a
                            則理事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的國際        true and fair view of the consolidated financial position of the
                            財務報告會計準則真實而中肯地反映 貴集團於         Group as at 31 December 2025 and of its consolidated financial
                            2025 年12 月31 日的綜合財務狀況及截至該日止   performance and its consolidated cash flows for the year then
                            年度的綜合財務表現和綜合現金流量，並已按照         ended in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards as issued
                            香港公司條例的披露規定妥為編製。              by the International Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”) and
                                                          have been properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure
                                                          requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

                            意見的基礎                         BASIS FOR OPINION
                             我們已根據香港會計師公會（「香港會計師公會」）      We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong




珍酒李渡集團有限公司 ZJLD GROUP INC
                             頒佈的香港審計準則（「香港審計準則」）進行審       Standards on Auditing (“HKSAs”) as issued by the Hong Kong
                             計。我們在該等準則下承擔的責任已在本報告核        Institute of Certified Public Accountants (the “HKICPA”). Our

珍酒李渡集團有限公司 ZJLD GROUP INC
                             key audit matter because revenue is a key performance
                             indicator of the Group and there is an inherent risk
                             that revenue may be manipulated to meet financial
                             expectations or targets. In addition, different types
                             of customers with different trade terms give rise to
                             complexity in timing of revenue recognition, and as a
                             result, revenue may not be properly recognised in the
                             correct periods.

                       138                                                                   財務報表附註
                                                   Notes to the financial statements
                                                                                                （除另有說明者外，以人民幣列示）
                                                                                      (Expressed in RMB unless otherwise indicated)

                            1   公司資料                                1   CORPORATE INFORMATION
                                珍 酒 李 渡 集 團 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」）於         ZJLD Group Inc (the “Company”) was incorporated in the
                                2021 年9 月24 日根據開曼群島公司法（經                Cayman Islands as an exempted company with limited
                                修訂）於開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限                      liability under the Companies Act (as revised) of the
                                公司。本公司股份於2023年4月27日在香                   Cayman Islands on 24 September 2021. The Company’s
                                港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上                    shares were listed on the Main Board of The Stock
                                市。本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）                    Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”)
                                主要從事釀造、生產及銷售白酒及其他酒                      on 27 April 2023. The Company and its subsidiaries
                                類產品。                                    (together, the “Group”) are principally engaged in

        (g)    信貸虧損及資產減值                             (g)   Credit losses and impairment of assets
               (i)    金融工具信貸虧損                             (i)   Credit losses from financial instruments
                      本集團就按攤銷成本計量的                               The Group recognises a loss allowance for
                      金融資產（包括銀行及手頭                               expected credit losses (“ECL”s) on financial
                      現金、貿易應收款項及應收                               assets measured at amortised cost (including
                      票據、按金及其他應收款                                cash at bank and on hand, trade and
                      項）確認預期信貸虧損（「預                              bills receivables, and deposits and other
                      期信貸虧損」）的虧損撥備。                              receivables).

                      預期信貸虧損的計量                                  Measurement of ECLs
                      預期信貸虧損為信貸虧損的                               ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate
                      概率加權估計。一般而言，                               of credit losses. Generally, credit losses
                      信貸虧損乃按所有預期現金                               are measured as the present value of all
                      差額（即合同金額與預期金                               expected cash shortfalls between the

               價及以合理成本取得融資，持續為                              continue to provide returns for shareholders and
               股東帶來回報及為其他持份者帶來                              benefits for other stakeholders, by pricing products
               利益。                                          and services commensurately with the level of risk
                                                            and by securing access to finance at a reasonable

               本集團積極並定期檢討及管理其資                              The Group actively and regularly reviews and
               本架構，以在不同借款水平可能帶                              manages its capital structure to maintain a balance
               來的較高股東回報與穩健資本狀況                              between the higher shareholders returns that might
               所帶來的優勢及保障之間取得平                               be possible with different levels of borrowings and
               衡，並就經濟狀況的變動對資本架                              the advantages and security afforded by a sound
               構作出調整。                                       capital position, and makes adjustments to the
                                                            capital structure in light of changes in economic
                                                            conditions.

               本公司或其任何附屬公司概不受外                              Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are
               部施加的資本要求所規限。                                 subject to externally imposed capital requirements.

               信貸風險指交易對手未履行其合約                              Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will
               責任而導致本集團產生財務虧損的                              default on its contractual obligations resulting in




                                                                                                                      珍酒李渡集團有限公司 ZJLD GROUP INC
               風險。本集團的信貸風險主要來自                              a financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit
               貿易應收款項。本公司董事認為，                              risk is primarily attributable to trade receivables.
               由於對手方為信譽良好的銀行及本                              The directors of the Company are of the opinion
               集團認為彼等的信貸風險較低，因                              that the Group’s exposure to credit risks arising
               此本集團因銀行及手頭現金、應收                              from cash at bank and on hand, bills receivables
               票據及應收利息（計入預付款項、                              and interest receivables (included in prepayments,
               按金及其他應收款項）面臨的信貸                              deposits and other receivables) is limited because
               風險有限。本集團並無提供將令本                              the counterparties are banks with good credit
               集團面臨信貸風險的任何擔保。                               standing, for which the Group considers to have
                                                            low credit risk. The Group does not provide any
                                                            guarantees which would expose the Group to
                                                            credit risk.
